\\\"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — Network Definitions\\\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\\\n# Changes: RSSM accepts variable dt via learned embedding; new EventGate class.\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport re\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom torch import nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nfrom torch import distributions as torchd\\\\n\\\\nimport tools\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass RSSM(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        stoch=30,\\\\n        deter=200,\\\\n        hidden=200,\\\\n        rec_depth=1,\\\\n        discrete=False,\\\\n        act=\\\\\\\"SiLU\\\\\\\",\\\\n        norm=True,\\\\n        mean_act=\\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\",\\\\n        std_act=\\\\\\\"softplus\\\\\\\",\\\\n        min_std=0.1,\\\\n        unimix_ratio=0.01,\\\\n        initial=\\\\\\\"learned\\\\\\\",\\\\n        num_actions=None,\\\\n        embed=None,\\\\n        device=None,\\\\n        max_dt=1,\\\\n        dt_embed_dim=32,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super(RSSM, self).__init__()\\\\n        self._stoch = stoch\\\\n        self._deter = deter\\\\n        self._hidden = hidden\\\\n        self._min_std = min_std\\\\n        self._rec_depth = rec_depth\\\\n        self._discrete = discrete\\\\n        act = getattr(torch.nn, act)\\\\n        self._mean_act = mean_act\\\\n        self._std_act = std_act\\\\n        self._unimix_ratio = unimix_ratio\\\\n        self._initial = initial\\\\n        self._num_actions = num_actions\\\\n        self._embed = embed\\\\n        self._device = device\\\\n\\\\n        self._max_dt = max_dt\\\\n        self._dt_embed_dim = dt_embed_dim\\\\n        if self._max_dt > 1 and dt_embed_dim > 0:\\\\n            self._dt_embedding = Conv2dSamePad(torch.nn.Conv2d):\\n    def calc_same_pad(self, i, k, s, d):\\n        return max((math.ceil(i / s) - 1) * s + (k - 1) * d + 1 - i, 0)\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        ih, iw = x.size()[-2:]\\n        pad_h = self.calc_same_pad(\\n            i=ih, k=self.kernel_size[0], s=self.stride[0], d=self.dilation[0]\\n        )\\n        pad_w = self.calc_same_pad(\\n            i=iw, k=self.kernel_size[1], s=self.stride[1], d=self.dilation[1]\\n        )\\n\\n        if pad_h > 0 or pad_w > 0:\\n            x = F.pad(\\n                x, [pad_w // 2, pad_w - pad_w // 2, pad_h // 2, pad_h - pad_h // 2]\\n            )\\n\\n        ret = F.conv2d(\\n            x,\\n            self.weight,\\n            self.bias,\\n            self.stride,\\n            self.padding,\\n            self.dilation,\\n            self.groups,\\n        )\\n        return ret\\n\\n\\nclass ImgChLayerNorm(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, ch, eps=1e-03):\\n        super(ImgChLayerNorm, self).__init__()\\n        self.norm = torch.nn.LayerNorm(ch, eps=eps)\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        x = x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)\\n        x = self.norm(x)\\n        x = x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)\\n        return x\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.54}}"}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TkR6EfAeoA4MxGLM85LKau","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\\\n\\\\nimport copy\\\\nimport math\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom torch import nn\\\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\\\n\\\\nimport networks\\\\nimport tools\\\\n\\\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\\\n\\\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\\\n    sigma = 1.0\\\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\\\n    return target_dist\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass RewardEMA:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"running mean and std\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\\\n        self.device = device\\\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\\\n\\\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\\\n        self._step = step\\\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\\\n        self._config = config\\\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\\\n            config.act,\\\\n            config.norm,\\\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\\\n            config.initial,\\\\n            config.num_actions,\\\\n            self.embed_size,\\\\n            config.device,\\\\n            max_dt=(2 if getattr(config, \\\\\\\"gate_type\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"gumbel\\\\\\\") == \\\\\\\"bernoulli_st\\\\\\\" else config.gate_max_dt) if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\\\n        )\\\\n        # plan_015B: freeze dt_emb to test if TRAINING (not existence) causes CIVO\\\\n        if getattr(config, 'dt_emb_freeze', False) and self.dynamics._dt_embedding is not None:\\\\n            self.dynamics._dt_embedding.weight.requires_grad_(False)\\\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\\\n        else:\\\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\\\n        self.heads[\\\\\\\"decoder\\\\\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.heads[\\\\\\\"reward\\\\\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\\\n            feat_size,\\\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\\\\\"dist\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"symlog_disc\\\\\\\" else (),\\\\n            config.reward_head[\\\\\\\"layers\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            config.units,\\\\n            config.act,\\\\n            config.norm,\\\\n            dist=config.reward_head[\\\\\\\"dist\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            outscale=config.reward_head[\\\\\\\"outscale\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            device=config.device,\\\\n            name=\\\\\\\"Reward\\\\\\\",\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.heads[\\\\\\\"cont\\\\\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\\\n            feat_size,\\\\n            (),\\\\n            config.cont_head[\\\\\\\"layers\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            config.units,\\\\n            config.act,\\\\n            config.norm,\\\\n            dist=\\\\\\\"binary\\\\\\\",\\\\n            outscale=config.cont_head[\\\\\\\"outscale\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            device=config.device,\\\\n            name=\\\\\\\"Cont\\\\\\\",\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        if config.gate_enabled:\\\\n            gate_type = getattr(config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel')\\\\n            if gate_type == 'bernoulli_st':\\\\n                self.event_gate = networks.BernoulliSTGate(\\\\n                    feat_size=feat_size,\\\\n                    hidden_units=config.gate_hidden_units,\\\\n                    hidden_layers=config.gate_hidden_layers,\\\\n                    act=config.act,\\\\n                    norm=config.norm,\\\\n                )\\\\n            else:\\\\n                self.event_gate = networks.EventGate(\\\\n                    feat_size=feat_size,\\\\n                    max_dt=config.gate_max_dt,\\\\n                    hidden_units=config.gate_hidden_units,\\\\n                    hidden_layers=config.gate_hidden_layers,\\\\n                    act=config.act,\\\\n                    norm=config.norm,\\\\n                    tau_init=config.gate_tau_init,\\\\n                    tau_final=config.gate_tau_final,\\\\n                    tau_anneal_steps=config.gate_tau_anneal_steps,\\\\n                )\\\\n            self.register_buffer(\\\\n                \\\\\\\"gate_step\\\\\\\", torch.tensor(0, dtype=torch.long)\\\\n            )\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self.event_gate = None\\\\n        self._effective_max_dt = 1.0\\\\n        self._gate_scale = 0.0\\\\n\\\\n        for name in config.grad_heads:\\\\n            assert name in self.heads, name\\\\n        self._model_opt = tools.Optimizer(\\\\n            \\\\\\\"model\\\\\\\",\\\\n            self.parameters(),\\\\n            config.model_lr,\\\\n            config.opt_eps,\\\\n            config.grad_clip,\\\\n            config.weight_decay,\\\\n            opt=config.opt,\\\\n            use_amp=self._use_amp,\\\\n        )\\\\n        print(\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Optimizer model_opt has {sum(param.numel() for param in self.parameters())} variables.\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        # other losses are scaled by 1.0.\\\\n        self._scales = dict(\\\\n            reward=config.reward_head[\\\\\\\"loss_scale\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            cont=config.cont_head[\\\\\\\"loss_scale\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def _update_gate_schedule(self):\\\\n        step = self._step\\\\n        phase1 = getattr(self._config, 'gate_curriculum_phase1', 25000)\\\\n        phase2 = getattr(self._config, 'gate_curriculum_phase2', 75000)\\\\n        if step < phase1:\\\\n            self._gate_scale = 0.0\\\\n        elif step < phase2:\\\\n            self._gate_scale = (step - phase1) / (phase2 - phase1)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self._gate_scale = 1.0\\\\n        gate_type = getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel')\\\\n        max_dt_eff = 2 if gate_type == 'bernoulli_st' else self._config.gate_max_dt\\\\n        self._effective_max_dt = 1 + self._gate_scale * (max_dt_eff - 1)\\\\n\\\\n    def _train(self, data):\\\\n        # action (batch_size, batch_length, act_dim)\\\\n        # image (batch_size, batch_length, h, w, ch)\\\\n        # reward (batch_size, batch_length)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": Writing config parameters...\n\nActually, I'm realizing there's a fundamental issue with sampling random transition pairs across episodes—the posterior state at any timestep depends on having processed the entire episode up to that point, so I can't just grab isolated pairs from different episodes. The current approach of processing full episodes and computing errors at every timestep within them should work fine for reproducibility as long as I use a fixed set of episodes with deterministic sorting and seed-based selection, then sample exactly the right number of transition pairs from those episodes.\n\nA better strategy would be to process all available episodes through the RSSM to get their posterior states, then sample the needed transitions from that full set. The concern is that processing all 112 episodes of length 501 on CPU could be slow—that's over 56,000 timesteps per model. But looking at the existing script, it only processes 15 episodes per model anyway, which gives roughly 7,500 valid transitions, so I could either stick with that subset or process all episodes and then sample down to the target number of transitions.\n\nThe key insight is to shift from averaging metrics per-episode to computing them at the individual transition level for better control. My approach: encode all episodes through the RSSM first, collect all valid transition indices for dt=2, then use a fixed seed to randomly sample 1000 pairs, and compute the metrics for each sampled transition.\n\nBut I'm realizing each model has its own encoder and RSSM parameters, so the posterior states differ per model even though we're using the same episodes and transition indices. The solution is to load the episodes once, sample the transition indices deterministically with the seed, then for each model separately encode the episodes, compute posteriors, and evaluate the sampled transitions before aggregating the results.\n\nActually, I need to reconsider—each model was trained on its own episodes from different policies, so evaluating trained_dt_emb on the baseline's data would introduce distribution shift. The existing script correctly evaluates each model on its own training episodes, which tests how well the RSSM models its own replay buffer rather than generalization across policies.\n\nFor the standardized script, I should follow the task specification where all checkpoints are passed via command line with corresponding labels, but I need to figure out how to handle the episodes—whether each checkpoint gets its own episode directory or if there's a shared set.\n\nLooking at the command structure, it seems each checkpoint directory should contain both the latest.pt model file and a train_eps/ folder with its episodes. The script will load episodes from each checkpoint's directory, sample the same number of transitions using the same seed for reproducibility across models, then evaluate RSSM fidelity. This keeps the comparison standardized even though each model uses its own data distribution.\n\nI also need to map each label to its corresponding model configuration—baseline has gating disabled with max_dt=1, trained_dt_emb has gating enabled with max_dt=8 and a 32-dimensional embedding, and the other variants follow similar patterns.\n\nNow I'm checking how the WorldModel initialization handles the max_dt parameter...\n\nFor the no_dt_emb configuration, max_dt gets set to 8 with dt_embed_dim=0, which means the RSSM won't create a dt_embedding layer. The baseline with gate_enabled=False sets max_dt=1 instead. Both end up with the same input layer size since neither has dt embeddings, but they differ in the max_dt attribute itself—which could matter for checkpoint compatibility even if the actual embedding is None in both cases.\n\nI should infer the model config from the checkpoint label and also allow an explicit --configs argument for flexibility. When the no_dt_emb model processes sequential rollouts at dt=2, it calls img_step twice with standard 1-step predictions since the dt parameter gets ignored when _dt_embedding is None, matching the baseline behavior exactly.\n\nFor the trained_dt_emb model, calling img_step without a dt parameter (or with dt=1) uses dt_embedding[0], and during sequential rollout at dt=2, we'd do two img_steps each with dt=1, which mirrors how the model performs sequential 1-step predictions in imagination. Though the existing script's approach of using a single img_step with the dt parameter is more aligned with actual practice during imagination, the task specification calls for sequential img_steps, so I'll follow that while also considering both methods for a more complete analysis.\n\nWriting the evaluation function... Building the episode tensors... Continuing with the prediction evaluation...\n\nNow I'm extracting the predicted observations and rewards from those features, then comparing them against the actual next timestep to compute the mean squared error for observations and mean absolute error for the reward prediction.\n\nThen I'm storing those error metrics and moving on to evaluate a two-step rollout where I apply two sequential actions to see how well the model predicts two timesteps ahead. Writing config JSON...\n\nActually, I'm realizing this per-transition approach is inefficient since I'm calling img_step repeatedly. The existing script batches transitions by episode, which is much faster. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_019vSTjftQpwj54AD14jQ6Pu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have a clear picture:\n\n- **exp_009d_baseline_walker_s1** (baseline): No dt_embedding, no gate keys → gate_enabled=False\n- **exp_008d_civo_trigger_s4** (trained_dt_emb): dt_embedding(8, 32), 27 gate keys → gate_enabled=True, max_dt=8, dt_embed_dim=32\n- **exp_009c_no_dt_emb_s2** (no_dt_emb): No dt_embedding, 27 gate keys → gate_enabled=True, max_dt=8, dt_embed_dim=0\n\n\n- **exp_084_frozen_dt_emb_walker_s0** (frozen_dt_emb): dt_embedding(8, 32), 27 gate keys → gate_enabled=True, max_dt=8, dt_embed_dim=32\n\nSo the frozen variant has the same architecture as the trained one, but the embedding weights aren't updated during training.\n\nFor the baseline, I need gate_enabled=False which gives max_dt=1 and no embedding layer. The no_dt_emb variant keeps the gate enabled with 27 keys but sets dt_embed_dim to 0, so the embedding stays None even though max_dt=8. The trained and frozen variants both use the full 8×32 embedding with the gate.\n\nNow I'm setting up the configuration dictionary for each model variant, specifying the gate settings that determine whether embeddings and gates get created.\n\nI need to think through the checkpoint loading carefully—if the baseline model was trained without event_gate, creating a new WorldModel with gate_enabled=False means that component won't exist at all, so strict loading will fail when trying to match the saved checkpoint.\n\nThe existing script handles this by extracting only the `_wm.` prefixed keys from the checkpoint and loading them with strict=True, which means it's loading just the world model portion while ignoring any agent-level keys.\n\nFor the baseline configuration with gate_enabled=False, there won't be any event_gate or gate_step buffers in either the checkpoint or the model, so the keys should align perfectly. For the other configurations with gate_enabled=True, the checkpoint will include those gate-related keys since they were present during training, and the model will expect them, so everything should match up correctly.\n\nNow I need to verify the gate_type configuration. The WorldModel defaults to 'gumbel' gate type unless explicitly set to 'bernoulli_st', and since the existing analysis script doesn't specify a gate_type, I'll assume all the models use the default gumbel implementation. \\\"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — Network Definitions\\\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\\\n# Changes: RSSM accepts variable dt via learned embedding; new EventGate class.\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport re\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom torch import nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nfrom torch import distributions as torchd\\\\n\\\\nimport tools\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass RSSM(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        stoch=30,\\\\n        deter=200,\\\\n        hidden=200,\\\\n        rec_depth=1,\\\\n        discrete=False,\\\\n        act=\\\\\\\"SiLU\\\\\\\",\\\\n        norm=True,\\\\n        mean_act=\\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\",\\\\n        std_act=\\\\\\\"softplus\\\\\\\",\\\\n        min_std=0.1,\\\\n        unimix_ratio=0.01,\\\\n        initial=\\\\\\\"learned\\\\\\\",\\\\n        num_actions=None,\\\\n        embed=None,\\\\n        device=None,\\\\n        max_dt=1,\\\\n        dt_embed_dim=32,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super(RSSM, self).__init__()\\\\n        self._stoch = stoch\\\\n        self._deter = deter\\\\n        self._hidden = hidden\\\\n        self._min_std = min_std\\\\n        self._rec_depth = rec_depth\\\\n        self._discrete = discrete\\\\n        act = getattr(torch.nn, act)\\\\n        self._mean_act = mean_act\\\\n        self._std_act = std_act\\\\n        self._unimix_ratio = unimix_ratio\\\\n        self._initial = initial\\\\n        self._num_actions = num_actions\\\\n        self._embed = embed\\\\n        self._device = device\\\\n\\\\n        self._max_dt = max_dt\\\\n        self._dt_embed_dim = dt_embed_dim\\\\n        if self._max_dt > 1 and dt_embed_dim > 0:\\\\n            self._dt_embedding = nn.Embedding(max_dt, dt_embed_dim)\\\\n            nn.init.normal_(self._dt_embedding.weight, std=0.01)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self._dt_embedding = None\\\\n\\\\n        inp_layers = []\\\\n        if self._discrete:\\\\n            inp_dim = self._stoch * self._discrete + num_actions\\\\n        else:\\\\n            inp_dim = self._stoch + num_actions\\\\n        if self._dt_embedding is not None:\\\\n            inp_dim += dt_embed_dim\\\\n        inp_layers.append(nn.Linear(inp_dim, self._hidden, bias=False))\\\\n        if norm:\\\\n            inp_layers.append(nn.LayerNorm(self._hidden, eps=1e-03))\\\\n        inp_layers.append(act())\\\\n        self._img_in_layers = nn.Sequential(*inp_layers)\\\\n        self._img_in_layers.apply(tools.weight_init)\\\\n        self._cell = GRUCell(self._hidden, self._deter, norm=norm)\\\\n        self._cell.apply(tools.weight_init)\\\\n\\\\n        img_out_layers = []\\\\n        inp_dim = self._deter\\\\n        img_out_layers.append(nn.Linear(inp_dim, self._hidden, bias=False))\\\\n        if norm:\\\\n            img_out_layers.append(nn.LayerNorm(self._hidden, eps=1e-03))\\\\n        img_out_layers.append(act())\\\\n        self._img_out_layers = nn.Sequential(*img_out_layers)\\\\n        self._img_out_layers.apply(tools.weight_init)\\\\n\\\\n        obs_out_layers = []\\\\n        inp_dim = self._deter + self._embed\\\\n        obs_out_layers.append(nn.Linear(inp_dim, self._hidden, bias=False))\\\\n        if norm:\\\\n            obs_out_layers.append(nn.LayerNorm(self._hidden, eps=1e-03))\\\\n        obs_out_layers.append(act())\\\\n        self._obs_out_layers = nn.Sequential(*obs_out_layers)\\\\n        self._obs_out_layers.apply(tools.weight_init)\\\\n\\\\n        if self._discrete:\\\\n            self._imgs_stat_layer = nn.Linear(\\\\n                self._hidden, self._stoch * self._discrete\\\\n            )\\\\n            self._imgs_stat_layer.apply(tools.uniform_weight_init(1.0))\\\\n            self._obs_stat_layer = nn.Linear(self._hidden, self._stoch * self._discrete)\\\\n            self._obs_stat_layer.apply(tools.uniform_weight_init(1.0))\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self._imgs_stat_layer = nn.Linear(self._hidden, 2 * self._stoch)\\\\n            self._imgs_stat_layer.apply(tools.uniform_weight_init(1.0))\\\\n            self._obs_stat_layer = nn.Linear(self._hidden, 2 * self._stoch)\\\\n            self._obs_stat_layer.apply(tools.uniform_weight_init(1.0))\\\\n\\\\n        if self._initial == \\\\\\\"learned\\\\\\\":\\\\n            self.W = torch.nn.Parameter(\\\\n                torch.zeros((1, self._deter), device=torch.device(self._device)),\\\\n                requires_grad=True,\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    def initial(self, batch_size):\\\\n        deter = torch.zeros(batch_size, self._deter, device=self._device)\\\\n        if self._discrete:\\\\n            state = dict(\\\\n                logit=torch.zeros(\\\\n                    [batch_size, self._stoch, self._discrete], device=self._device\\\\n                ),\\\\n                stoch=torch.zeros(\\\\n                    [batch_size, self._stoch, self._discrete], device=self._device\\\\n                ),\\\\n                deter=deter,\\\\n            )\\\\n        else:\\\\n            state = dict(\\\\n                mean=torch.zeros([batch_size, device=self._device),\\\\n                deter=deter,\\\\n            )\\\\n        if self._initial == \\\\\\\"zeros\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return state\\\\n        elif self._initial == self.get_stoch(state[\\\\\\\"deter\\\\\\\"])\\\\n            return state\\\\n        else:\\\\n            raise NotImplementedError(self._initial)\\\\n\\\\n    def observe(self, embed, action, \"v3_standardized\"\nCODE_DIR = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon\")\nsys.path.insert(0, str(CODE_DIR))\n\nimport models\nimport networks\nimport tools\n\nLABEL_CONFIGS = {\n    \"baseline\": dict(gate_enabled=False, gate_max_dt=8, gate_dt_embed_dim=0),\n    \"trained_dt_emb\": dict(gate_enabled=True, gate_max_dt=8, gate_dt_embed_dim=32),\n    \"no_dt_emb\": dict(gate_enabled=True, gate_max_dt=8, gate_dt_embed_dim=0),\n    \"frozen_dt_emb\": dict(gate_enabled=True, gate_max_dt=8, gate_dt_embed_dim=32),\n}\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoints\", required=True, help=\"Comma-separated checkpoint dirs\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--labels\", required=True, help=\"Comma-separated model labels\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_samples\", type=int, default=1000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task\", required=True)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", required=True)\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cpu\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef detect_obs_config(episodes_dir):\n    files = sorted(Path(episodes_dir).glob(\"*.npz\"))\n    ep = dict(np.load(files[0]))\n    skip = {\"action\", \"discount\", \"image\", \"is_first\", \"is_terminal\", \"logprob\", \"reward\"}\n    keys = sorted(k for k in ep if k not in skip)\n    shapes = OrderedDict((k, tuple(ep[k].shape[1:])) for k in keys)\n    return keys, shapes, ep[\"action\"].shape[-1]\n\n\nclass FakeSpace:\n    def __init__(self, d):\n        self.spaces = OrderedDict((k, SimpleNamespace(shape=v)) for k, v in d.items())\n\n\ndef make_config(device, num_actions, gate_enabled, gate_max_dt=8, gate_dt_embed_dim=32):\n    return SimpleNamespace(\n        device=device, precision=32,\n        dyn_hidden=512, dyn_deter=512, dyn_stoch=32, dyn_discrete=32,\n        dyn_rec_depth=1, dyn_mean_act=\"none\", dyn_std_act=\"sigmoid2\",\n        dyn_min_std=0.1, unimix_ratio=0.01, initial=\"learned\",\n        num_actions=num_actions, units=512, act=\"SiLU\", norm=True,\n        encoder=dict(\n            mlp_keys=\".*\", cnn_keys=\"$^\", act=\"SiLU\", norm=True,\n            cnn_depth=32, kernel_size=4, minres=4,\n            mlp_layers=5, mlp_units=1024, symlog_inputs=True,\n        ),\n        decoder=dict(\n            mlp_keys=\".*\", cnn_keys=\"$^\", act=\"SiLU\", norm=True,\n            cnn_depth=32, kernel_size=4, minres=4,\n            mlp_layers=5, mlp_units=1024, cnn_sigmoid=False,\n            image_dist=\"mse\", vector_dist=\"symlog_mse\", outscale=1.0,\n        ),\n        reward_head=dict(layers=2, dist=\"symlog_disc\", loss_scale=1.0, outscale=0.0),\n        cont_head=dict(layers=2, loss_scale=1.0, outscale=1.0),\n        grad_heads=[\"decoder\", \"reward\", \"cont\"],\n        model_lr=1e-4, opt_eps=1e-8, grad_clip=1000, weight_decay=0.0, opt=\"adam\",\n        gate_enabled=gate_enabled, gate_max_dt=gate_max_dt,\n        gate_dt_embed_dim=gate_dt_embed_dim,\n        gate_hidden_units=256, gate_hidden_layers=2,\n        gate_tau_init=5.0, gate_tau_final=0.5, gate_tau_anneal_steps=200000,\n        gate_loss_weight=1.0,\n        gate_entropy_bonus_init=0.1, gate_entropy_bonus_final=0.01,\n        gate_curriculum_phase1=50000, gate_curriculum_phase2=150000,\n        discount=0.997,\n    )\n\n\ndef load_world_model(ckpt_path, obs_shapes, config):\n    wm = models.WorldModel(\n        FakeSpace(obs_shapes),\n        SimpleNamespace(shape=(config.num_actions,)),\n        0, config,\n    )\n    ckpt = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location=config.device)\n    wm_state = {k[4:]: v for k, v in ckpt[\"agent_state_dict\"].items()\n                if k.startswith(\"_wm.\")}\n    wm.load_state_dict(wm_state, strict=True)\n    wm.eval()\n    return wm\n\n\ndef load_episodes(d):\n    return [dict(np.load(f)) for f in sorted(Path(d).glob(\"*.npz\"))]\n\n\ndef sample_transitions(episodes, n, seed):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    pairs = []\n    for i, ep in enumerate(episodes):\n        T = ep[\"action\"].shape[0]\n        for t in range(T - 2):\n            pairs.append((i, t))\n    pairs = np.array(pairs)\n    actual_n = min(n, len(pairs))\n    idx = rng.choice(len(pairs), size=actual_n, replace=False)\n    idx.sort()\n    return [(int(pairs[i][0]), int(pairs[i][1])) for i in idx]\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef evaluate_model(wm, episodes, samples, device, obs_keys):\n    has_dt_emb = wm.dynamics._dt_embedding is not None\n    ep_to_ts = defaultdict(list)\n    for ep_idx, t in samples:\n        ep_to_ts[ep_idx].append(t)\n\n    dt1_obs, dt1_rew = [], []\n    dt2_obs, dt2_rew = [], []\n    dt1_pk = {k: [] for k in obs_keys}\n    dt2_pk = {k: [] for k in obs_keys}\n    n_eps = len(ep_to_ts)\n\n    for i, (ep_idx, timesteps) in enumerate(sorted(ep_to_ts.items())):\n        ep = episodes[ep_idx]\n        obs = {k: torch.tensor(ep[k], dtype=torch.float32, device=device).unsqueeze(0)\n               for k in obs_keys}\n        action = torch.tensor(ep[\"action\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device).unsqueeze(0)\n        is_first = torch.tensor(ep[\"is_first\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device).unsqueeze(0)\n        reward = torch.tensor(ep[\"reward\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device).unsqueeze(0)\n\n        embed = wm.encoder(obs)\n        post, _ = wm.dynamics.observe(embed, action, is_first)\n\n        ts = torch.tensor(sorted(timesteps), dtype=torch.long)\n        N = len(ts)\n        post_batch = {k: v[0, ts] for k, v in post.items()}\n        act_t = action[0, ts]\n        act_t1 = action[0, ts + 1]\n\n        if has_dt_emb:\n            dt_one = torch.ones(N, dtype=torch.long, device=device)\n            pred1 = wm.dynamics.img_step(post_batch, act_t, dt=dt_one, sample=False)\n        else:\n            pred1 = wm.dynamics.img_step(post_batch, act_t, sample=False)\n\n        feat1 = wm.dynamics.get_feat(pred1).unsqueeze(0)\n        dec1 = wm.heads[\"decoder\"](feat1)\n        rew1 = wm.heads[\"reward\"](feat1)\n\n        for k in obs_keys:\n            tgt = obs[k][0, ts + 1].unsqueeze(0)\n            mse = ((dec1[k].mode() - tgt) ** 2).mean(dim=-1).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            dt1_pk[k].append(mse)\n\n        dt1_obs.append(np.stack([dt1_pk[k][-1] for k in obs_keys]).mean(0))\n        r1 = rew1.mode()\n        if r1.dim() == 3:\n            r1 = r1.squeeze(-1)\n        dt1_rew.append((r1.squeeze(0) - reward[0, ts + 1]).abs().cpu().numpy())\n\n        # dt=2: sequential rollout (two img_steps)\n        if has_dt_emb:\n            pred2 = wm.dynamics.img_step(pred1, act_t1, dt=dt_one, sample=False)\n        else:\n            pred2 = wm.dynamics.img_step(pred1, act_t1, sample=False)\n\n        feat2 = wm.dynamics.get_feat(pred2).unsqueeze(0)\n        dec2 = wm.heads[\"decoder\"](feat2)\n        rew2 = wm.heads[\"reward\"](feat2)\n\n        for k in obs_keys:\n            tgt = obs[k][0, ts + 2].unsqueeze(0)\n            mse = ((dec2[k].mode() - tgt) ** 2).mean(dim=-1).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            dt2_pk[k].append(mse)\n\n        dt2_obs.append(np.stack([dt2_pk[k][-1] for k in obs_keys]).mean(0))\n        r2 = rew2.mode()\n        if r2.dim() == 3:\n            r2 = r2.squeeze(-1)\n        dt2_rew.append((r2.squeeze(0) - reward[0, ts + 2]).abs().cpu().numpy())\n\n        if (i + 1) % 20 == 0 or i == n_eps - 1:\n            print(f\"    [{i+1}/{n_eps}] episodes done\")\n\n    dt1_obs_arr = np.concatenate(dt1_obs)\n    dt2_obs_arr = np.concatenate(dt2_obs)\n    dt1_rew_arr = np.concatenate(dt1_rew)\n    dt2_rew_arr = np.concatenate(dt2_rew)\n    d1pk = {k: float(np.mean(np.concatenate(v))) for k, v in dt1_pk.items()}\n    d2pk = {k: float(np.mean(np.concatenate(v))) for k, v in dt2_pk.items()}\n\n    return {\n        \"dt1\": {\n            \"obs_mse\": float(np.mean(dt1_obs_arr)),\n            \"obs_mse_std\": float(np.std(dt1_obs_arr)),\n            \"obs_mse_per_key\": d1pk,\n            \"reward_mae\": float(np.mean(dt1_rew_arr)),\n            \"reward_mae_std\": float(np.std(dt1_rew_arr)),\n        },\n        \"dt2\": {\n            \"obs_mse\": float(np.mean(dt2_obs_arr)),\n            \"obs_mse_std\": float(np.std(dt2_obs_arr)),\n            \"obs_mse_per_key\": d2pk,\n            \"reward_mae\": float(np.mean(dt2_rew_arr)),\n            \"reward_mae_std\": float(np.std(dt2_rew_arr)),\n        },\n        \"ratio\": {\n            \"obs\": float(np.mean(dt2_obs_arr)) / max(float(np.mean(dt1_obs_arr)), 1e-10),\n            \"reward\": float(np.mean(dt2_rew_arr)) / max(float(np.mean(dt1_rew_arr)), 1e-10),\n            \"per_key\": {k: d2pk[k] / max(d1pk[k], 1e-10) for k in obs_keys},\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    ckpt_dirs = [d.strip() for d in args.checkpoints.split(\",\")]\n    labels = [l.strip() for l in args.labels.split(\",\")]\n    assert len(ckpt_dirs) == len(labels), \\\n        f\"Mismatch: {len(ckpt_dirs)} checkpoints vs {len(labels)} labels\"\n\n    first_eps = Path(ckpt_dirs[0]) / \"train_eps\"\n    obs_keys, obs_shapes, num_actions = detect_obs_config(first_eps)\n    print(f\"obs_keys={obs_keys}, shapes={dict(obs_shapes)}, num_actions={num_actions}\")\n\n    all_results = OrderedDict()\n\n    for ckpt_dir, label in zip(ckpt_dirs, labels):\n        ckpt_path = Path(ckpt_dir) / \"latest.pt\"\n        eps_dir = Path(ckpt_dir) / \"train_eps\"\n\n        if not ckpt_path.exists():\n            print(f\"SKIP {label}: no checkpoint at {ckpt_path}\")\n            all_results[label] = {\"error\": \"checkpoint not found\",\n                                  \"checkpoint_path\": str(ckpt_path)}\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n=== {label} ===\")\n        mcfg = LABEL_CONFIGS.get(label, LABEL_CONFIGS[\"trained_dt_emb\"])\n        config = make_config(args.device, num_actions, **mcfg)\n\n        try:\n            wm = load_world_model(ckpt_path, obs_shapes, config)\n            episodes = load_episodes(eps_dir)\n            samples = sample_transitions(episodes, args.n_samples, args.seed)\n            print(f\"  {len(episodes)} eps, {len(samples)} samples, \"\n                  f\"dt_emb={wm.dynamics._dt_embedding is not None}\")\n\n            res = evaluate_model(wm, episodes, samples, args.device, obs_keys)\n            res[\"exp_id\"] = Path(ckpt_dir).name\n            res[\"checkpoint_path\"] = str(ckpt_path)\n            res[\"checkpoint_size\"] = ckpt_path.stat().st_size\n            res[\"n_episodes\"] = len(episodes)\n            res[\"has_dt_embedding\"] = wm.dynamics._dt_embedding is not None\n            all_results[label] = res\n\n            print(f\"  dt1: obs_mse={res['dt1']['obs_mse']:.6f}  \"\n                  f\"reward_mae={res['dt1']['reward_mae']:.4f}\")\n            print(f\"  dt2: obs_mse={res['dt2']['obs_mse']:.6f}  \"\n                  f\"reward_mae={res['dt2']['reward_mae']:.4f}\")\n            print(f\"  ratio: obs={res['ratio']['obs']:.2f}x  \"\n                  f\"reward={res['ratio']['reward']:.2f}x\")\n            del wm\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            all_results[label] = {\"error\": str(e),\n                                  \"checkpoint_path\": str(ckpt_path)}\n\n    output = {\n        \"metadata\": {\n            \"script_version\": SCRIPT_VERSION,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n            \"n_samples\": args.n_samples,\n            \"task\": args.task,\n            \"device\": args.device,\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            \"obs_keys\": obs_keys,\n            \"obs_shapes\": {k: list(v) for k, v in obs_shapes.items()},\n            \"num_actions\": num_actions,\n            \"method\": \"sequential_rollout\",\n        },\n        \"models\": dict(all_results),\n    }\n\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n Given the high CPU utilization (~40 cores), processing should be fast. Let me wait a full 5 minutes from start.\n\nActually wait, I think maybe the elapsed time format is different. `01:56` in `etime` means `1 hour 56 minutes` if there's no leading hour digit... \n\nNo, it should be `MM:SS`. Let me just wait 5 minutes and check if the file is done. Looking at the etime format more carefully, it's `MM:SS` for processes under an hour, `HH:MM:SS` for those over an hour, and `D-HH:MM:SS` for more than a day. So 01:56 is 1 minute 56 seconds, which makes sense given my checks are separated by several seconds and the elapsed time only increased by about 40 seconds between them.
